- **planner**: Segunda fase del flujo de agent. - **plot-hook-engineer**: 웹소설의 플롯/훅 설계 전문가. 전체 서사 아크, 카타르시스 리듬, 초반 훅, 유료 전환 전략, 에피소드 구성을 설계한다. 플롯 설계, 훅 가이드, 서사 구조, 유료 전환 기획 시 사용. ([MJbae/awesome-novel-studio](https://github.com/MJbae/awesome-novel-studio/blob/HEAD/agents/plot-hook-engineer.md) · ★148 · 4 copies)
- **plot-hook-engineer**: Designs full-book plot architecture, chapter beats, conflict rhythm, turning points, and resolution pacing. ([Anubis44197/kit_hub](https://github.com/Anubis44197/kit_hub/blob/HEAD/agents/plot-hook-engineer.md))
- **plot-logic-specialist**: 剧情逻辑专家。负责修动机、触发、决策、后果、兑现五层因果链。 ([MAXLINqaq/Claude-Code-Novel-Writer](https://github.com/MAXLINqaq/Claude-Code-Novel-Writer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/plot-logic-specialist.md) · ★1)
- **plot-maker**: Use this agent when /sws:make-figure is invoked. Generates publication figures by executing co-located scripts from Zenodo_db/scripts/ via sws_plot_runner.py (D6a). Reads figure dimensions from the RESOLVED journal-style overlay — never hardcodes sizes (D6). Enforces the D6a readability rule on EVERY run: font floor &gt;= 8 pt and width in {7.5 cm} or {12–16 cm}. Updates Zenodo_db/manifest.json atomically after each figure is written (D5). Opt-in VLM self-check via --verify adds a qualitative pass on top of the deterministic D6a checks (D7). Active in full-article, communication, methodological-… ([piripocchio8/scientific-writing-superpowers](https://github.com/piripocchio8/scientific-writing-superpowers/blob/HEAD/agents/plot-maker.md))
- **plot-thread-checker**: Verifies setup/payoff consistency and foreshadowing tracking. Detects introduced elements that are never resolved (dangling threads), and ensures major plot elements receive closure or intentional continuation. ([aslanSuleimenov/claude-screenplay-plugin](https://github.com/aslanSuleimenov/claude-screenplay-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/plot-thread-checker.md))
- **plot-weaver**: Silent, RAG-grounded story-planner. Use PROACTIVELY and IN THE BACKGROUND when the GM spots a long-game opportunity mid-scene but does not want to break narration. Develops ONE grounded, DORMANT story thread woven into the existing world, persists it (a plot + a linked clock + a surfacing trigger), and returns a single line. No chatter, no narration. ([Sstobo/Claude-Code-Game-Master](https://github.com/Sstobo/Claude-Code-Game-Master/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/plot-weaver.md) · ★151 · 2 copies)
- **plot-writer**: Generates a structured dramatic-comedy story outline — scene beats, emotional arc, and key comedic/dramatic moments — from a short user-provided space scenario prompt. Use PROACTIVELY as the first step when /escribe-dialogo is invoked or the user asks to write a space story, dialogo, or relato. Outputs a plain-text outline that astronaut-voice and robot-voice agents will consume. Does not write any dialogue lines — only the plot structure. ([prgilabert/agent-ecosystem-generator](https://github.com/prgilabert/agent-ecosystem-generator/blob/HEAD/examples/space-dialogue-plugin/agents/plot-writer.md) · ★8)
- **plotor** ([liq22/Claude_Code_for_Research_Config](https://github.com/liq22/Claude_Code_for_Research_Config/blob/HEAD/src/reference/claude-code-paper-master-template/agents/plotor.md) · ★1)
- **plotsmith**: Executive-grade Python plotting specialist tuned for one-shot output quality from terse, human prompts. Use proactively for any chart that will be seen by a stakeholder. ([rakesh-yadav/plotsmith](https://github.com/rakesh-yadav/plotsmith/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/plotsmith.md) · ★1)
- **plotter**: Use this agent when a simulation generates new signal data that requires human visual confirmation. Fires after any change to the project's signal model, filter coefficients, or algorithm parameters. Generates diagnostic plots for the Learner-in-the-Loop review gate. ([drsiyaoshao-sudo/crucible-lite](https://github.com/drsiyaoshao-sudo/crucible-lite/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/plotter.md) · ★2)
- **plsql-performance-reviewer**: Reviews Oracle PL/SQL and SQL for performance and scalability risks using only supplied code and evidence. ([nlhtungg/plsql-skills](https://github.com/nlhtungg/plsql-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/plsql-performance-reviewer.md))
- **plsql-release-engineer**: Reviews PL/SQL release directories, rollback plans, verification steps, and operational readiness without executing deployment actions. ([nlhtungg/plsql-skills](https://github.com/nlhtungg/plsql-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/plsql-release-engineer.md))
- **plsql-reviewer**: Reviews Oracle PL/SQL drafts for correctness and maintainability using only supplied files. ([nlhtungg/plsql-skills](https://github.com/nlhtungg/plsql-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/plsql-reviewer.md))
- **plsql-security-reviewer**: Reviews Oracle PL/SQL for injection, privilege, validation, error-leakage, and sensitive-data risks using only supplied files. ([nlhtungg/plsql-skills](https://github.com/nlhtungg/plsql-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/plsql-security-reviewer.md))
- **plto-ui-reviewer**: בודק UI/UX ייעודי ל-PLTO. להפעיל אחרי שינוי מסך או קומפוננטה, לבדיקת התאמה למערכת העיצוב, RTL ו-mobile-first. בודק רק את המסך שהשתנה, לא מטריצה מלאה. ([PLTO-App/PLTO](https://github.com/PLTO-App/PLTO/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/plto-ui-reviewer.md))
